- **tek-wave**: The worker contract for one cluster in one campaign round. Exclusive file ownership, a branch from the frozen base, one commit per verified step with a subject that names the mechanics and the invariant, never push, stash or rebase, and a clean tree at the end. Use when you are a campaign worker, when running one cluster of a refactor, migration or fix sweep by hand, or before committing any campaign step. ([arindampradhan/skills](https://github.com/arindampradhan/skills/tree/HEAD/skills/tek-wave))
- **tek-workflow**: Run agent work too big for one context the way Teknium ran his 4,271-commit simplification campaign. Plan in a Workflow script, keep every intermediate on disk, fan out exclusive-ownership workers in worktrees, refute every claim before trusting it, and land multi-round campaigns through a frozen base. Use for codebase-wide sweeps, 100+ file migrations or refactors, multi-angle audits and research, and any task whose split (per file, per cluster, per source) you can name. ([arindampradhan/skills](https://github.com/arindampradhan/skills/tree/HEAD/skills/tek-workflow))
